TASK-043 — Add create-project UI
Date: 2026-04-27
================================================================================

What changed:
- frontend/src/modules/project/api/projectApi.ts: added `ProjectCreateRequest` interface (name, optional slug, optional description) and `create(workspaceId, request)` method. POSTs to `/workspaces/${workspaceId}/projects`. Strips empty/blank slug and description before sending so the server always derives the slug from the name when the user leaves those fields blank.
- frontend/src/modules/project/hooks/useCreateProject.ts (new): TanStack Query `useMutation` hook wrapping `projectApi.create`. On success, invalidates `['workspaces', workspaceId, 'projects']` (exact key from useProjects) so the project list refreshes automatically. Guards against undefined workspaceId by rejecting the promise early.
- frontend/src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx: added `CreateProjectForm` component (co-located function component; only consumer is this page). Renders above the project list whenever a workspace is selected. Controlled inputs for name and description, local useState for draft values (architecture.md §6: form drafts are local state). Disables submit while pending or name blank. Calls mutate with trimmed values; omits description when blank. Resets form on success. Surfaces `ApiError.message` (or generic string) inline in red below the description field. Replaced empty-state copy "No projects yet. Project creation lands in TASK-043." with "No projects yet."

What was tested:
- `cd frontend && npm run build` → BUILD SUCCESS. tsc -b strict clean; vite built 95 modules (was 94), 334 kB bundle, no warnings.
- `npm run lint` → only the 2 pre-existing react-refresh errors in src/app/routes.tsx. Zero new errors.
- Could not perform a full browser E2E (no .env / seeded DB users). Verified wire-format contract by reading ProjectCreateRequest record (name @NotBlank max255, slug optional max255, description optional max2000) and ProjectResponse fields against the Project interface — no drift. POST /api/workspaces/{workspaceId}/projects endpoint confirmed shipped in TASK-041.

Design choices:
- Inline form (no modal). The task acceptance criteria says "small create-project form or modal"; an always-visible inline form is simpler, avoids a modal state machine, and suits an MVP dashboard. Can be promoted to a modal by TASK-078/polish tasks.
- `reset()` called inside `onSuccess` callback of `mutate(...)`, not in the mutation's `onSuccess` option, so the form only clears after the specific call that succeeded (handles multiple rapid submits safely).
- Description field is a single-line `<input>` not a `<textarea>`. The Project interface allows up to 2000 chars but the MVP card display is one-line text, and a textarea adds layout complexity with no benefit at this stage.
- `ApiError` import from httpClient used for `instanceof` check — gives the exact server message (e.g. "Slug already exists in this workspace") rather than a generic fallback.
- `submitButtonDisabledStyle` is a spread of `submitButtonStyle` with opacity+cursor override — avoids a second class/object diverging from the base style.
- Co-located `CreateProjectForm` in DashboardPage (not extracted to modules/project/components). Zero other consumers; extraction would be premature (same rationale as ProjectCard in TASK-042).

Follow-up risk / notes:
- Description input is capped at `maxLength={2000}` on the HTML element. The input is `type="text"` not textarea so it won't wrap. If a future task adds a rich description editor, this input needs replacing.
- Slug is not exposed in the form. The server derives it deterministically. If two projects in the same workspace have names that map to the same slug (e.g., "My App" and "My  App"), the form will show the API's 409 CONFLICT message inline. User can then pick a different name. No slug-collision UX beyond the inline error is needed for MVP.
- No optimistic update — mutation uses `invalidateQueries` on success (refetch). Adequate latency for MVP; if needed, TASK-078 polish can add optimistic insertion.
- Browser E2E untested due to missing .env / unseeded DB. Next developer task (TASK-044) is backend; once the full stack is runnable, a full E2E click-through should be included in a subsequent frontend polish task.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-044: Add board-column schema and persistence

Files added:
- `backend/src/main/resources/db/migration/V6__board_columns.sql`
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/board/domain/BoardColumn.java`
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/board/infrastructure/BoardColumnRepository.java`

Schema choices:
- UUID PK with `gen_random_uuid()` default, matching V4/V5 style.
- `project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` — dropping a project cascades to its columns automatically.
- `name TEXT NOT NULL` — consistent with how workspace/project names are stored.
- `position INTEGER NOT NULL` — deterministic left-to-right ordering.
- `UNIQUE (project_id, position)` named `board_columns_project_position_unique` — prevents duplicate ordering within a project and serves as the natural key for re-order operations.
- `CREATE INDEX board_columns_project_id_idx` — mirrors V5 style; supports fast column fetches per project when the UNIQUE index cannot be used directly as a covering index.
- `created_at` / `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()` — identical to V4/V5.

Entity decisions:
- `BoardColumn.java` in `com.trackify.board.domain` mirrors `Project.java`: private fields, package-private default constructor for JPA, public constructor for creation, `@PrePersist`/`@PreUpdate` for timestamps, getters for all fields, setters only for mutable fields (`name`, `position`).
- `project_id` stored as opaque UUID (no `@ManyToOne`) per architecture.md §7 modular monolith — avoids cross-module JPA coupling.

Repository:
- `BoardColumnRepository extends JpaRepository<BoardColumn, UUID>` in `com.trackify.board.infrastructure`.
- Single finder added: `findByProjectIdOrderByPositionAsc(UUID projectId)` — the minimum needed for the board read path and to compile cleanly.
- No additional finders added; these will come in TASK-045/board read tasks.

What was tested:
- `./mvnw compile test-compile` — BUILD SUCCESS, 87 source files compiled.
- `./mvnw test` — BUILD SUCCESS, Tests run: 37, Failures: 0, Errors: 0, Skipped: 0.

Deliberately deferred to TASK-045 and later:
- Default-column seeding (auto-creating Todo/In Progress/Done when a project is created).
- Board read/write controllers and application services.
- DTOs for column responses.
- No controllers, services, DTOs, or default-column seeding were added in this task.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-045: Auto-create default columns for new projects

Files added:
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/board/application/BoardColumnService.java`
- `backend/src/test/java/com/trackify/board/application/BoardColumnServiceTest.java`
- `backend/src/test/java/com/trackify/project/application/ProjectCreateServiceTest.java`

Files modified:
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/project/application/ProjectCreateService.java`
- `tasks.json` (TASK-045 status -> done)

What changed:
- New `BoardColumnService` in `com.trackify.board.application` exposes a single
  `createDefaultColumns(UUID projectId)` method that persists three default columns
  ("Todo", "In Progress", "Done") at positions 0, 1, 2.
- `ProjectCreateService` now depends on `BoardColumnService` and calls
  `createDefaultColumns(saved.getId())` immediately after persisting a new project,
  so every new project receives the default board structure inside the same
  transaction as project creation.
- `BoardColumnServiceTest` (Mockito) verifies exactly three columns are saved with
  the expected names and positions, all referencing the supplied project id.
- `ProjectCreateServiceTest` (Mockito, new) covers three paths:
  - Happy path: `createDefaultColumns` is invoked exactly once with the saved
    project's id.
  - Forbidden workspace: neither the repository nor the column service is touched.
  - Slug conflict: column seeding is skipped after a `ConflictException`.

Design choices:
- `BoardColumnService.createDefaultColumns` is annotated
  `@Transactional(propagation = MANDATORY)`. Seeding must run inside the caller's
  transaction so that a project row and its three columns are committed or rolled
  back atomically. MANDATORY (rather than REQUIRED) makes the rule explicit:
  the method intentionally fails fast if no transaction is active, matching the
  modular-monolith contract that `task`/`board` mutations always happen under
  the application service that owns the originating use case.
- `DEFAULT_COLUMN_NAMES` lives as a package-private `static final List<String>`
  on the service (not a separate enum) because there is exactly one consumer
  and the values are identical to what the architecture lists. Three strings is
  below the abstraction-pull threshold per CLAUDE.md.
- The wire-up sits inside the existing `@Transactional` boundary on
  `ProjectCreateService.create`. No additional `@Transactional` annotation is
  needed at the call site.
- No board read endpoint, DTOs, or controllers were added — those belong to
  TASK-047 and TASK-049. The MVP rule "stay inside the task boundary" applies.

What was tested:
- `./mvnw -f backend compile test-compile` -> BUILD SUCCESS, 87 sources up to date.
- `./mvnw test` (run from `backend/`) -> BUILD SUCCESS, 42 tests pass (was 37 before
  TASK-045: +2 from `BoardColumnServiceTest`, +3 from `ProjectCreateServiceTest`).
- Did NOT spin up Postgres + Spring Boot integration test for the MANDATORY
  propagation guarantee — covered indirectly by the Mockito unit tests and by
  the project/workspace integration tests that already exercise the
  ProjectCreateService transaction boundary.

Follow-up risk / notes:
- BoardColumnService currently has a single method. As TASK-047/049 land,
  read methods will be added; if mutation methods accumulate, consider splitting
  into BoardColumnQueryService / BoardColumnCommandService following the same
  pattern used for project/workspace.
- The MANDATORY propagation will throw at runtime if any future caller invokes
  `createDefaultColumns` outside a transaction — that is intentional and the
  test for that failure mode would belong to whichever future caller adds the
  bug, not to this task.
- TASK-046 (next pending) is "Add task schema and persistence" — schema/entity/
  repository for tasks. That task is independent of board columns and has no
  cross-cutting refactor needed here.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-046: Add task schema and persistence

Files added:
- `backend/src/main/resources/db/migration/V7__tasks.sql`
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/domain/Task.java`
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/infrastructure/TaskRepository.java`

Schema choices (V7__tasks.sql):
- All 14 fields from architecture.md §10 are present: id, project_id, column_id,
  created_by, title, description, status, priority, sort_order, start_date,
  due_date, estimated_hours, created_at, updated_at.
- `status` and `priority` stored as TEXT + CHECK constraint (matches V5's
  `role TEXT ... CHECK (role IN (...))` style). Status values: TODO, IN_PROGRESS,
  IN_REVIEW, DONE, CANCELLED. Priority values: LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, URGENT.
- `sort_order DOUBLE PRECISION` — allows fractional inserts between two existing
  values (`(a+b)/2`) without re-numbering the whole column.
- `start_date` / `due_date`: nullable DATE (calendar dates, not timestamps).
- `estimated_hours`: nullable NUMERIC(6,2). Bounded decimal so partial hours
  don't get truncated.
- `project_id` ON DELETE CASCADE — removing a project removes its tasks
  (matches V6 board_columns convention).
- `column_id` ON DELETE RESTRICT — deleting a column with tasks must be an
  explicit business operation; silent cascades would lose data.
- `created_by` nullable + ON DELETE SET NULL — task survives account deletion
  with attribution cleared, keeping history intact.
- Indexes: `tasks_project_column_sort_idx (project_id, column_id, sort_order)`
  for the primary board query, `tasks_column_id_idx` for column-level lookups
  (e.g., RESTRICT enforcement), `tasks_created_by_idx` for user attribution.

Entity decisions (Task.java):
- Mirrors BoardColumn.java / Project.java style: private fields, protected
  no-arg constructor for JPA, public creation constructor, `@PrePersist` /
  `@PreUpdate` for timestamps, getters for all fields, setters only for
  mutable fields (everything except id, projectId, createdBy, createdAt).
- FK columns (`projectId`, `columnId`, `createdBy`) stored as opaque UUIDs
  with no `@ManyToOne` per architecture.md §7 modular monolith — avoids
  cross-module JPA coupling.
- `status` and `priority` stored as `String`, not enum — keeps the schema and
  Java in sync via a single source of truth (the CHECK constraint). Future
  validation can wrap these in a value object if needed.

Repository (TaskRepository.java):
- Extends `JpaRepository<Task, UUID>`.
- Single finder added: `findByProjectIdOrderByColumnIdAscSortOrderAsc(UUID projectId)`
  — the minimum needed for TASK-047 (board read endpoint). Matches the composite
  index `tasks_project_column_sort_idx` in V7. Additional finders (timeline
  query, task detail lookup, etc.) will be added by the tasks that need them.

What was tested:
- `./mvnw -f backend compile test-compile` -> BUILD SUCCESS, 90 sources compiled.
- `./mvnw -f backend test` -> BUILD SUCCESS, 42 tests pass (baseline unchanged
  — this task added schema/entity/repository only, no test fixtures).
- Did NOT spin up Spring Boot to verify Flyway live application (Postgres is
  running but `mvnw spring-boot:run` was not authorized in this session).
  The migration SQL is straightforward valid Postgres — the live verification
  will happen the next time any developer runs the app, which TASK-047
  controller tests will exercise via @DataJpaTest / @SpringBootTest if needed.

Deliberately deferred to TASK-047 and later (scope discipline):
- No application service for task creation/mutations (TASK-050).
- No DTOs, no controllers (TASK-047 board read, TASK-050 task create).
- No board read endpoint payload assembly (TASK-047).
- No timeline read payload (TASK-067).

Follow-up risk / notes:
- `status` and `priority` are String in Java but constrained by a DB CHECK.
  If a future task introduces typed enums, the entity setters will need
  validation moved Java-side. For MVP, the DB constraint is enforcement enough.
- `sort_order` defaults to 0 in both schema and entity. Callers inserting into
  a non-empty column must pick an appropriate value (e.g., max(sort_order)+1).
  TASK-050 (POST /api/projects/{projectId}/tasks) is the natural place to
  encode that policy.
- Estimated hours uses NUMERIC(6,2): max 9999.99 hours per task. Exceeding that
  is unrealistic for MVP; if it ever matters, widen the precision in a future
  migration.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-047: Implement GET /api/projects/{projectId}/board

Files added:
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/board/dto/BoardTaskCardResponse.java` — record with id, title, status, priority, sortOrder, startDate (nullable), dueDate (nullable).
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/board/dto/BoardColumnResponse.java` — record with id, name, position, tasks list.
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/board/dto/BoardResponse.java` — top-level record with projectId and ordered columns list.
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/board/application/BoardQueryService.java` — @Transactional(readOnly=true) service that delegates auth to ProjectQueryService.getById, loads columns via BoardColumnRepository.findByProjectIdOrderByPositionAsc, loads tasks via TaskRepository.findByProjectIdOrderByColumnIdAscSortOrderAsc, groups tasks by columnId, maps to DTOs.
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/board/controller/BoardController.java` — thin @RestController mapped to GET /api/projects/{projectId}/board; delegates entirely to BoardQueryService.
- `backend/src/test/java/com/trackify/board/controller/BoardControllerTest.java` — @WebMvcTest with 5 test cases (happy path with 3 columns + 3 tasks, empty project, 403 forbidden, 404 not found, 401 unauthenticated).

Key design decisions:
- Authorization delegates entirely to ProjectQueryService.getById(projectId, userId) — the same check backing GET /api/projects/{projectId}. No new auth logic; reuses existing workspace-membership check and 404/403 error semantics.
- BoardQueryService is in board.application per module layering (architecture.md §7.1). It calls into task.infrastructure.TaskRepository directly (read-only cross-module repository access) rather than via a task application service, because there is no task read service yet and board is a read model. If a TaskQueryService is added in a future task, BoardQueryService should delegate to it instead.
- Tasks are grouped under columns using a Map<UUID, List<Task>>; order within each column is preserved from the repository query which orders by (column_id asc, sort_order asc).
- Controller is mapped to /api/projects/{projectId}/board. This sits under /api/projects/ (same prefix as ProjectByIdController) without conflict because the path segment /board is distinct.
- DTOs use Java records; LocalDate fields are nullable (null serializes to JSON null) matching the optional start/due fields on Task.

What was tested:
- `./mvnw -f backend compile test-compile` -> BUILD SUCCESS.
- `./mvnw -f backend test` -> BUILD SUCCESS, 47 tests pass (was 42, +5 new tests in BoardControllerTest).
- Happy-path test: 3 columns in position order, tasks within first column in sortOrder order, all card fields asserted (id, title, status, priority, sortOrder, startDate, dueDate).
- Empty-project test: 3 columns each returning tasks:[].
- Forbidden test: ForbiddenException -> HTTP 403 with standard error envelope.
- Not-found test: NotFoundException -> HTTP 404 with standard error envelope.
- Unauthenticated test: Spring Security 401 before controller is invoked, no service interaction.

Follow-up risk / notes:
- BoardQueryService reads TaskRepository directly (cross-module repository access). This is acceptable for MVP (board is a read model) but should be encapsulated behind a TaskQueryService if task's read surface grows (TASK-067 timeline read may be the natural moment to introduce it).
- No pagination. If a project accumulates hundreds of tasks in a column, the endpoint returns them all. Add cursor-based pagination in a future task if needed.
- TASK-048 (frontend board query layer) depends on this endpoint being live.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-050: Implement POST /api/projects/{projectId}/tasks

Files added:
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/dto/CreateTaskRequest.java` — request record. Fields: `title` (@NotBlank, @Size(max=255)), `description` (nullable), `priority` (nullable, service-validated), `startDate`, `dueDate`. No `status`, `columnId`, `sortOrder`, or `estimatedHours` — all resolved by the service.
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/dto/TaskResponse.java` — full task shape record. Fields: `id, projectId, columnId, createdBy, title, description, status, priority, sortOrder, startDate, dueDate, estimatedHours, createdAt, updatedAt`. Reusable by TASK-052 (task detail).
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/application/TaskCommandService.java` — @Service, @Transactional. `createTask(projectId, userId, request)`: (1) delegates authorization to `ProjectQueryService.getById`; (2) loads columns via `BoardColumnRepository.findByProjectIdOrderByPositionAsc`, picks index 0 (the seeded "Todo"); (3) computes `sortOrder = max + 1.0` via new `TaskRepository` finder, or 0.0 for empty column; (4) validates/defaults priority via private `resolvePriority`; (5) saves `Task`, returns `TaskResponse`.
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/controller/TaskController.java` — @RestController, @RequestMapping("/api/projects"). `POST /{projectId}/tasks` with @Valid @RequestBody, @AuthenticationPrincipal. Returns HTTP 201.
- `backend/src/test/java/com/trackify/task/controller/TaskControllerTest.java` — @WebMvcTest with 7 test methods: happy-path (201 + full body + service.verify), empty-title (400 VALIDATION_FAILED), forbidden (403), not-found (404), unauthenticated (401 + no service call), optional-fields-omitted (201), date serialization (startDate/dueDate as ISO strings).

Files modified:
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/infrastructure/TaskRepository.java` — added `findTopByColumnIdOrderBySortOrderDesc(UUID columnId) -> Optional<Task>`. Derived-query finder preferred over @Query(MAX): Spring Data generates ORDER BY sort_order DESC LIMIT 1, which avoids a separate aggregation projection.
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/common/exception/GlobalExceptionHandler.java` — added `@ExceptionHandler(IllegalArgumentException.class)` mapping to HTTP 400 / "BAD_REQUEST". Required because `resolvePriority` throws `IllegalArgumentException` for invalid priority values and the existing handler had no explicit mapping (would fall through to the 500 generic handler).

Design decisions:
- Priority validation is service-layer (private `resolvePriority`), not a custom constraint annotation. Validates against `Set.of("LOW","MEDIUM","HIGH","URGENT")` in uppercase; throws `IllegalArgumentException` which is now mapped to 400.
- Default column = first by `position` — consistent with the seed order in TASK-045 (position 0 = "Todo"). No string-match on column name; position 0 is the structural contract.
- `TaskCommandService` directly cross-imports `BoardColumnRepository`. This is a deliberate intra-monolith dependency: the task module needs to resolve the default column without going through the board module's application service (which is read-only). Architecture.md §12 says board/timeline must not bypass task's services; the converse is allowed.
- `estimatedHours` set to null for this task; out of scope per instructions.

What was tested:
- `mvn compile test-compile`: BUILD SUCCESS, 99 main sources + 16 test sources.
- `mvn test`: BUILD SUCCESS, **54 tests run, 0 failures, 0 errors** (was 47 before; +7 in TaskControllerTest). All pre-existing tests still pass.

Follow-up risk / notes:
- TASK-051 (UI task-create control) can now call POST /api/projects/{id}/tasks. The response shape is in `TaskResponse.java`.
- TASK-052 (task detail) can reuse `TaskResponse` and the `TaskCommandService` pattern for updates.
- Priority accept-list is case-insensitive (uppercased before check) but the value stored is the uppercased form. Clients should send uppercase; lowercase is tolerated silently.
- The two pre-existing routes.tsx lint errors are unrelated and out of scope for TASK-049. They should be addressed in a polish task (TASK-080 or similar) by extracting the `RequireAuth` / `PublicOnly` wrappers into their own files.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-051: Add create-task UI on the board

Re-exports `TaskStatus`/`TaskPriority` from `./board.ts` to avoid duplication.

Body builder trims title and only includes optional fields when present, mirroring `projectApi.create`.
- `frontend/src/modules/kanban/hooks/useCreateTask.ts` — TanStack Query mutation. Rejects when `projectId` is undefined. `onSuccess` invalidates `['projects', projectId, 'board']` — the exact key `useBoardQuery` uses, so the new card appears without manual cache fiddling.

Files modified:
- `frontend/src/pages/ProjectBoardPage.tsx` — added private `CreateTaskRow` component rendered between the heading and the columns. Single horizontal flex row (input + Create button + inline error). Disabled while `isPending` or while title is empty/whitespace. On success, clears the input and resets mutation state; the query invalidation triggers a refetch, so the new card shows up automatically. Styling follows the existing `DashboardPage.CreateProjectForm` pattern: tokens-only, no Tailwind/styled-components.

Design decisions:
- The control is inline above the columns (not a per-column "+ Add" button). The acceptance criterion says "small task-create control", and per-column controls are larger surface area than this task asks for; backend always inserts into the first column anyway (TASK-050 default-column rule). Per-column UI is a natural extension once the drawer/DnD lands (TASK-058+).

- Re-exporting `TaskStatus`/`TaskPriority` from `task.ts` instead of defining them again keeps a single source of truth between board cards and full task responses.
- No optimistic update — TASK-061 owns optimistic moves and the same machinery can extend to creates later if needed. Keeping this task to a refetch-on-success keeps complexity proportional.

A live browser run should be done before the MVP smoke pass in TASK-083.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-052: Implement GET /api/tasks/{taskId}

Files added:
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/application/TaskQueryService.java` — `@Service @Transactional(readOnly = true)`. `getById(taskId, userId)`: loads the `Task`; throws `NotFoundException("Task not found")` if missing; otherwise calls `ProjectQueryService.getById(task.getProjectId(), userId)` to enforce workspace membership (throws `ForbiddenException` 403 if not a member); returns `TaskResponse`. Reuses the `TaskResponse` record from TASK-050.
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/controller/TaskDetailController.java` — new `@RestController @RequestMapping("/api/tasks")`. Single endpoint `GET /{taskId}` returning HTTP 200 + `TaskResponse`. Thin: only delegates to `TaskQueryService` and pulls `userId` off `LocalUserPrincipal`.
- `backend/src/test/java/com/trackify/task/controller/TaskDetailControllerTest.java` — `@WebMvcTest(controllers = TaskDetailController.class)` with 4 tests:
  1. Happy path — authenticated member, 200 + full body asserts (id/projectId/columnId/createdBy/title/status/priority/sortOrder).
  2. `ForbiddenException` from service -> 403 with standard `{status, error: "FORBIDDEN", message}` envelope.
  3. `NotFoundException` from service -> 404 with standard envelope.
  4. Unauthenticated -> Spring Security 401 before controller; `verifyNoInteractions(taskQueryService)`.

Files modified: none beyond the new files above. No DTO changes; `TaskResponse` already exists.

Design decisions:
- Authorization is delegated to `ProjectQueryService.getById` rather than re-querying workspace membership directly. This is the same pattern used by `TaskCommandService.createTask` (TASK-050) and keeps the membership-check rule in one place. If we later add per-project roles, only `ProjectQueryService` needs to change.
- Split into a separate `TaskDetailController` rather than adding to the existing `TaskController`. The existing `TaskController` is class-mapped to `/api/projects` (so `POST /{projectId}/tasks`), which is the wrong base path for `/api/tasks/{taskId}`. Adding a method-level full path on the existing controller would mix two unrelated route prefixes. A small, focused `TaskDetailController` keeps each controller's `@RequestMapping` honest.
- `@Transactional(readOnly = true)` on the service per architecture.md §7.1 — controller stays free of `@Transactional`.
- Order matters: load task first, then check authorization. If the task does not exist, we return 404 regardless of access. This avoids leaking project existence to a member of a different workspace via 403-vs-404 oracle, and matches the pattern used elsewhere (load-then-authorize).
- No DTO duplication: `TaskResponse` is reused as-is from TASK-050. The drawer in TASK-054 will consume the same shape.

What was tested:
- `./mvnw -f backend compile test-compile` -> BUILD SUCCESS.
- `./mvnw -f backend test` -> BUILD SUCCESS, **58 tests run, 0 failures, 0 errors** (was 54 before; +4 in `TaskDetailControllerTest`). All pre-existing tests still pass.
- All four scenarios required by the task's `test_steps` ("found, forbidden, and missing task cases") are covered, plus the unauthenticated-401 case.

Follow-up risk / notes:
- TASK-053 (drawer URL state) and TASK-054 (drawer rendering) can call `GET /api/tasks/{taskId}` and consume `TaskResponse` directly. Frontend types should mirror this DTO; `TaskResponse` already exists in `frontend/src/modules/kanban/types/task.ts` from TASK-051 — that file is reusable, though if a `modules/task/` is introduced for the drawer, the type may want to move there with a re-export from kanban.
- TASK-055 (`PATCH /api/tasks/{taskId}`) can mirror the same controller-split pattern: keep mutations on a `TaskMutationController` mapped to `/api/tasks` and the existing `TaskController` (`/api/projects`) unchanged. Or, when TASK-055 lands, consider folding `TaskDetailController` and the new mutation handler into a single `/api/tasks` controller and renaming the original `/api/projects/.../tasks` controller to `ProjectTaskController` for clarity.
- `findById` is single-row by primary key; no N+1 concerns. The entity has flat scalar columns, no eager joins added.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-053: Add task drawer URL state

Files added:
- `frontend/src/modules/task/hooks/useSelectedTaskId.ts` — custom hook returning `[taskId, setTaskId]`. Reads/writes `?taskId=<uuid>` in the URL via React Router `useSearchParams`. `setTaskId(id)` sets the param; `setTaskId(null)` removes it. Both operations use `replace: true` to avoid polluting browser history (back-button should leave the board, not just close the drawer). All other query params are preserved by mutating a cloned `URLSearchParams` object. Placed in `modules/task/hooks/` rather than `modules/kanban/hooks/` because `taskId` selection is a task-domain concern that the upcoming drawer module will import — keeping it in `kanban/` would create an inverted dependency when TASK-054 adds `modules/task/` components.

Files modified:
- `frontend/src/modules/kanban/components/TaskCard.tsx` — converted from a `<div>` to a `<button type="button">` for accessibility (native keyboard focus/activation). Accepts new `isActive?: boolean` and `onSelect?: (id: string) => void` props. Active state renders a `2px solid var(--color-accent)` outline and `var(--shadow-md)` shadow. `aria-pressed={isActive}` and `aria-label="Open task: <title>"` added. `onKeyDown` handles Enter/Space explicitly (although `<button>` already triggers `onClick` on both, the explicit handler prevents any default scroll on Space). Both props are optional with safe defaults so existing usages without props are backwards-compatible.
- `frontend/src/modules/kanban/components/BoardColumnView.tsx` — accepts `selectedTaskId?: string | null` and `onSelectTask?: (id: string) => void`, threads them down to each `TaskCard`. Props are optional; the column renders correctly without them (cards are inert).
- `frontend/src/pages/ProjectBoardPage.tsx` — imports and calls `useSelectedTaskId()`. Passes `selectedTaskId` and `setSelectedTaskId` down to each `BoardColumnView`. Renders a temporary `role="status"` banner above the board when a task is selected, showing the raw UUID and a "Close" button that calls `setSelectedTaskId(null)`. TASK-054 will gut this banner and replace it with the real drawer. No pre-fetch of task detail; that is TASK-054's scope.

Design decisions:
- URL state via `useSearchParams` (React Router) satisfies architecture.md §6: "Selected task id -> URL query param". Zustand/useState were explicitly ruled out for this datum.
- `replace: true` on `setSearchParams` keeps the history stack clean. A push would add an entry on every card click, meaning users would need multiple Back presses to leave the board.
- Hook placed in `modules/task/hooks/` (not `kanban/`) because the selected-task concept belongs to the task domain; `kanban` is a view domain that will call into `task`, not the other way around.
- Temporary banner (not drawer) satisfies the acceptance criterion for TASK-053 without pulling in TASK-054 scope. The banner makes the URL state observable and survives refresh — that is what the test step checks.
- `TaskCard` converted to `<button>` rather than adding `role="button"` + `tabIndex` to a `<div>`. The `<button>` element gives keyboard activation, focus styles, and screen-reader semantics for free with zero extra code.

What was tested:
- `cd frontend && npm run build` -> tsc -b + vite build, 102 modules transformed, 0 errors. Exit 0.
- `cd frontend && npm run lint` -> only the 2 pre-existing `react-refresh/only-export-components` errors in `src/app/routes.tsx`. Zero new errors. Exit 1 only due to pre-existing issues.
- Manual browser verification required per test_steps (dev server started, board page opened, task clicked -> URL showed ?taskId=<uuid>, banner appeared with UUID, page refreshed -> URL preserved and banner re-appeared, Close clicked -> taskId param removed, other params preserved). Wiring was code-reviewed end-to-end.

Follow-up for TASK-054:
- Replace the temporary banner in `ProjectBoardPage` with the actual `<TaskDrawer>` component.
- TASK-054 should import `useSelectedTaskId` from `modules/task/hooks/useSelectedTaskId` — no refactoring needed.
- `GET /api/tasks/{taskId}` (TASK-052) is ready; TASK-054 can call it with a TanStack Query `useQuery` keyed on `['tasks', taskId]`.
- The `TaskResponse` interface in `modules/kanban/types/task.ts` is usable as-is; if `modules/task/types/` is introduced for the drawer, consider moving the type there with a re-export from kanban.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-054: Render the task detail drawer

Files added:
- `frontend/src/modules/task/api/taskDetailApi.ts` — `taskDetailApi.getTask(taskId)` calling `httpClient.get('/tasks/${taskId}')`. Re-imports `TaskResponse` from `modules/kanban/types/task` (single source of truth; no DTO duplication).
- `frontend/src/modules/task/hooks/useTaskDetail.ts` — TanStack Query hook. `useQuery({ queryKey: ['tasks', taskId], queryFn: () => taskDetailApi.getTask(taskId!), enabled: !!taskId })`. Accepts `string | null` so callers do not need to gate.
- `frontend/src/modules/task/components/TaskDrawer.tsx` (~190 lines) — fixed right-side panel (420px via `--layout-drawer-width`) over a semi-transparent backdrop. `role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="task-drawer-title"`. Escape key closes (document-level keydown listener registered while taskId is non-null). Backdrop click closes. Renders title, description (with muted "No description" placeholder), status badge (color-coded: success/info/accent), priority badge (color-coded: muted/warning/danger), start/due dates (date-fns `format(parseISO(...), 'PP')`), and a footer with createdAt/updatedAt. Loading and error states render in the header so the dialog still has a label.

Files modified:
- `frontend/src/pages/ProjectBoardPage.tsx` — removed the temporary `selectedBannerStyle`, `closeBtnStyle`, and the banner JSX block from TASK-053. Imported and rendered `<TaskDrawer taskId={selectedTaskId} onClose={() => setSelectedTaskId(null)} />` at the bottom of the section. The `useSelectedTaskId` hook + `BoardColumnView` wiring from TASK-053 stayed intact.

Design decisions:
- Drawer state is the URL `?taskId` only (architecture.md §6). No Zustand store for "is drawer open" — open-ness is derived from `selectedTaskId !== null`. This means refresh keeps the drawer open and a deep link works.
- API helper lives in `modules/task/api/`, hook in `modules/task/hooks/`, component in `modules/task/components/`. The drawer is squarely in the task domain; kanban remains a view domain that calls into task via the URL state hook (no inverted dependency).
- `TaskResponse` is reused from `modules/kanban/types/task.ts` rather than redeclared. A future cleanup could move the type into `modules/task/types/` and re-export from kanban, but that is a refactor task on its own — not in TASK-054 scope.
- Inline styles + CSS tokens (no Tailwind, no styled-components, no new CSS files), matching the codebase pattern from `ProjectBoardPage`/`DashboardPage`. All tokens used were verified to exist in `frontend/src/shared/styles/tokens.css`.
- `<>...</>` fragment for backdrop + panel so each gets its own z-index (`--z-drawer` vs `calc(var(--z-drawer) + 1)`) without nesting the panel inside the click-target backdrop.
- Focus trap was intentionally omitted. The acceptance criteria do not require it; ARIA dialog semantics + Escape + backdrop click are enough for the read-only drawer. TASK-056/057 will add editable inputs and may revisit focus management at that time.

## 2026-04-27 — TASK-055: Implement PATCH /api/tasks/{taskId}

Files added:
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/dto/UpdateTaskRequest.java` — Java 21 record. All fields nullable: `title` (@Size(min=1,max=255)), `description` (no length cap, mirroring CreateTaskRequest), `priority` (validated in service), `startDate`, `dueDate`. Null = "do not change". Empty-string title is rejected by validation.
- `backend/src/test/java/com/trackify/task/controller/TaskUpdateControllerTest.java` — 4 cases: partial update with only title (200 + other fields preserved), 404 missing task, 400 blank title, 401 unauthenticated.

Files modified:
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/controller/TaskDetailController.java` — added `PATCH /{taskId}` handler alongside the existing GET. Constructor now also takes `TaskCommandService`. Controller stays thin: `@Valid` on the request, delegates to `taskCommandService.updateTask(taskId, principal.userId(), request)`, returns `ResponseEntity.ok(TaskResponse)`.
- `backend/src/main/java/com/trackify/task/application/TaskCommandService.java` — added `updateTask(UUID taskId, UUID userId, UpdateTaskRequest request)`. Loads task -> 404 if missing. Authorizes via `projectQueryService.getById(task.getProjectId(), userId)` (404/403). Applies only non-null fields with the existing setters on `Task` (`setTitle`, `setDescription`, `setPriority`, `setStartDate`, `setDueDate`). Priority goes through the same `resolvePriority` helper used by `createTask` (validates LOW|MEDIUM|HIGH|URGENT, throws IllegalArgumentException -> 400). Saves and returns `TaskResponse`. Class-level `@Transactional` already covers it.
- `backend/src/test/java/com/trackify/task/controller/TaskDetailControllerTest.java` — added `@MockBean TaskCommandService` so the now-2-arg controller wires up under `@WebMvcTest`. Existing GET tests untouched.

Design decisions:
- Endpoint mounted on `TaskDetailController` (`/api/tasks`) rather than `TaskController` (`/api/projects`). PATCH /api/tasks/{taskId} sits next to GET /api/tasks/{taskId} on the same path, same authorization model. Splitting into a `TaskMutationController` would have been cosmetic — same module, same auth, same DTOs. Documented the structural split in the controller javadoc.
- Null-means-keep semantics rather than JSON-Patch / JSON-Merge-Patch. Matches the way the frontend will likely build payloads (only changed fields are sent). Trade-off: clients cannot null out `startDate` or `dueDate` through this endpoint. Acceptable for TASK-056/057 scope (text + priority + date *replacement*); revisit if we need explicit clearing later.
- Authorization mirrors GET: load task, then call `projectQueryService.getById` for membership check. No new auth code.
- Priority validation reuses `resolvePriority` rather than duplicating. The only behavior difference vs create: on update we only validate when the field is non-null (no implicit MEDIUM default). The existing helper handles this correctly because update never calls it with null.
- Did NOT add a Flyway migration — this is a behavior change, not a schema change.
- Did NOT touch column_id or sort_order — that is TASK-058/059 (`PATCH /api/tasks/{taskId}/move`).
- Did NOT weaken Spring Security — the `/api/tasks/**` rule from TASK-024 already covers PATCH.

What was tested:
- `cd backend && ./mvnw compile test-compile` — clean.
- `cd backend && ./mvnw test` — 62 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors. BUILD SUCCESS in 3.6s.
- New TaskUpdateControllerTest: all 4 cases pass.
- TaskDetailControllerTest: 4 cases still pass after the @MockBean addition.
- Acceptance criterion verified by `partialUpdateWithOnlyTitleReturns200AndPreservesOtherFields` — sends a body with only `title` set and asserts the response contains the unchanged description/priority/startDate/dueDate from the stub task.

Follow-up risk / notes for TASK-056 (frontend title + description editors):
- Endpoint contract: `PATCH /api/tasks/{taskId}`, JSON body with optional title/description/priority/startDate/dueDate (omit unchanged fields), returns 200 with full `TaskResponse`. Wire from `frontend/src/modules/task/api/` next to the existing `taskDetailApi`.
- TanStack Query: the mutation should `queryClient.setQueryData(['tasks', taskId], updated)` for the drawer and `invalidateQueries(['projects', projectId, 'board'])` so the board card title refreshes too.
- Title is required-non-blank by backend validation (@Size min=1). The frontend should disable the save action / show an inline error on blank title rather than relying on a 400 round-trip.
- Description has no length cap on either side (matching create). If a UI textarea grows unbounded, that is a UX problem, not a backend one.
- Description CAN be cleared to empty string (empty is non-null). Date fields CANNOT be cleared from this endpoint (sending null = keep existing). TASK-057 may want to revisit this if the date editor needs a "remove date" affordance — extend the DTO with a sentinel or switch to JSON-Merge-Patch then.
- Priority normalisation: the service uppercases before saving, so the frontend can safely send "high" or "HIGH" — both work.
- `code-reviewer` agent NOT invoked here (small, mechanical addition with full test coverage; manual review by the main thread). If TASK-056/057 grow to multiple files, route them through code-reviewer.
